Optimization details:
We can implement a smooth objective with log-sum-exp and gradient. To improve speed, use **precomputed lag indices**? Previous gradient loops over 2n-1 lags; for n=1000, 2000 operations plus vector slices; maybe okay if maxiter few? But L-BFGS will call gradient hundreds times. 2000*1000 operations ~2M vector ops per grad, maybe ~0.1s? 100 grads 10s. n=500 ~6s. Previous may spend more in SLSQP. We can vectorize gradient further but maybe not necessary. However we can implement efficient gradient via **circulant/correlation with weights** using np.convolve once. Let's derive a simpler formula to compute gradient as combination of convolutions of weight array w (length 2n-1) with h and 1-h.

Given full lag weights w_idx for lags l = idx - (n-1), total C = ∑ w_l C_l where w includes dx. We need gradient ∑_l w_l ∂C_l.
Can express all lags (positive and negative) in a unified formula using arrays of weights W_plus[k] for k≥0 and W_minus[k] for k<0. But we can construct two weight arrays P and N as above.
Let P be length n, P[d] = w_{+d} * dx (d=0..n-1) for positive lags (including 0). Let N be length n, N[d] = w_{-d} * dx (d=1..n-1), N[0]=0 for negative lags (d>0).
Then gradient:
F1 = ∑_{d=0}^{n-1-j} P[d] (1 - h[j+d])  # P cross-correlate forward with a=1-h.
F2 = ∑_{d=1}^{j} N[d] (1 - h[j-d])  # N backward with a.
H1 = ∑_{d=0}^{j} P[d] h[j-d]  # P backward with h.
H2 = ∑_{d=1}^{n-1-j} N[d] h[j+d]  # N forward with h.
G = F1 + F2 - H1 - H2.
We can compute via np.convolve:
- For sequence a, cross_forward(P,a): out[j] = ∑_{d≥0, j+d<n} P[d] a[j+d]. This is np.correlate(a, P)[:n]? Let's test: np.correlate(a, v, 'full')[n-1+j]? Definition: full[k]=∑_m a[m] v[m-k+i]? Better use known: np.convolve(P_rev, a) maybe. For out[j]=∑_{d=0}^{n-j-1} P[d] a[j+d]. Let b=P reversed? Convolution (c = P ⋆ a)[j] = ∑_{m} P[m] a[j-m]. If P nonzero m=d, this includes a[j-d] (backward), not forward. Cross-corr (a, P)[j]? np.correlate(a, P, 'full') shift. Let's derive using convolution with reversed P: Let Pr = P[::-1], length n. Convolve Pr and a, take indices n-1 -? conv[Pr,a][r]=∑_m Pr[m] a[r-m]. Need r=n-1-j => ∑_m P[n-1-m] a[n-1-j - m]. Let d=n-1-m => P[d] a[j+d -? wait n-1-j - (n-1-d)=d-j? Not.
Let's use scipy.signal.fftconvolve with a flipped kernel for forward:
For forward F1[j] = ∑_{d=0}^{L-1} P[d] a[j+d]. Let kernel Q of length n with Q[n-1-d]=P[d]? Convolve a with Q: (a * Q)[j+n-1] = ∑_m a[m] Q[j+n-1-m]. Let m=j+d. Then Q[n-1-d]=P[d]. Good. So F1 = convolve(a, Q_forward)[n-1 : n-1+n]. Where Q_forward[n-1-d]=P[d]. In numpy: Q = np.empty(n); Q[n-1::-1]=P. Then F1 = np.convolve(a,Q,mode='full')[n-1:2n-1]. But direct convolve O(n^2); use fftconvolve. For n up to 2000 okay.
Backward F2[j]=∑_{d=1}^j N[d] a[j-d]. This is convolution of a with kernel R where R[d]=N[d] (for d=1..n-1) and R[0]=0? (a * R)[j] = ∑_m a[m] R[j-m], with m=j-d => R[d]=N[d]. So F2 = convolve(a, R, 'full')[:n] (since indices j=0..n-1). Similar H1 = convolve(h, S)[:n] with P[d] (including d=0) backward; H2 forward with N: T[n-1-d]=N[d] (d>=1), convolve(h,T)[n-1:2n-1].
Total G = conv(fwd P,a)+conv(away N,a)-conv(back P,h)-conv(fwd N,h). We can combine kernels to reduce from 4 to 4 convolutions? F1+H1 involve P; F2+H2 involve N. P: G_P = F1(P,a) - H1(P,h). Since a=1-h, F1(P,1-h)-F1(P,h) minus? Actually H1=P*h. F1(P,1-h) = F1(P,1) - F1(P,h). F1(P,1) with a=1 is ∑_{d=0}^{n-j-1} P[d] = suffix sum of P (including d=0). So G_P = suffixP[j] - F1(P,h) - H1(P,h). That's 2 convolutions (forward P*h and backward P*h) plus suffix. Good!
For N: G_N = F2(N,1-h) - H2(N,h) = F2(N,1) - F2(N,h) - H2(N,h). F2(N,1) = prefix sum of N (d=1..j). Let prefixN[j]. Then G_N = prefixN[j] - (forward? F2 is backward N*h) - (H2 forward N*h). Specifically F2=backward N*h = conv(h,R_N)[:n]; H2=forward N*h = conv(h,T_N)[n-1:]. So G_N = prefixN - backward_N*h - forward_N*h.
Thus total gradient = dx included in P,N: G = suffixP[j] + prefixN[j] - (fwdP*h + backP*h + fwdN*h + backN*h). Wait signs: H1=backP*h, F1(P,h)=fwdP*h, so subtract both; H2 subtract both. Good.
Let's test for k=0 only: P[0]=w0 dx, N=0. suffixP=w0dx all? P only at d=0, suffixP[j]=w0. fwdP*h = w0 h, backP*h = w0 h. G=w0 -2w0 h = w0(1-2h), correct. For positive lag only w_d (d>0): P[d]=w. suffixP[j]=w if j+d<n else0. fwdP*h[j]=w h[j+d] if j+d<n. backP*h[j]=w h[j-d] if j>=d. G = w[(1-h[j+d]) - h[j-d]] matches: for j≤n-1-d first w(1-h[j+d]); for j≥d second -w h[j-d]. Good. For negative lag -a: N[a]=w. prefixN[j]=w if j≥a else0. backN*h[j]=w h[j-a] if j≥a. fwdN*h[j]=w h[j+a] if j+a<n. G = w[(1? wait C_{-a}= w[(1-h[j-a]) for j≥a - h[j+a] for j+a<n]. Our G_N = prefixN - backN - fwdN = w[1 if j≥a] - w h[j-a] - w h[j+a]. That's w(1 - h[j-a]) - w h[j+a], correct. Good! Great.

So gradient can be computed with four convolutions of length n with weight arrays derived from P,N plus prefix/suffix. We can use direct np.convolve for small n, or scipy.signal.fftconvolve for larger. But P and N derived from the max weights w, which are many. P length n with P[d]=w_{center+d} dx if lag positive; N[d]=w_{center-d} dx for lag -d. Weight array w is softmax over C vector. We can choose to use log-sum-exp weights normalized with alpha. Convolution implementation top-level:
def grad_from_weights(w, h):
  n=len(h); dx=2/n; center=n-1
  P = np.zeros(n); N=np.zeros(n)
  # positive d 0..n-1: idx=center+d
  P[0:n] = w[center:center+n] * dx
  # negative d 1..n-1: idx=center-d => w[0:center]
  N[1:n] = w[0:center][::-1] * dx? Need N[d] for d=1..n-1 corresponds lag -d = idx center-d. w idx from 0 to center-1 correspond d=center-idx = n-1-idx. For d=n-1: idx=0. So N[n-1] = w[0] dx. N[1] = w[center-1] dx. So N[1:] = w[center-1::-1] * dx.
  a = h
  suffixP = np.cumsum(P[::-1])[::-1]
  prefixN = np.cumsum(N) - N[0]? N[0]=0 so prefixN = np.cumsum(N)
  Need convs:
   backP = convolve(h, P, 'full')[:n] because kernel P[d] same as backward (m=j-d). np.convolve(h,P) index r = ∑ h[m]P[r-m], m=j-d, d=r-m, P[d] => ok for r=0..n-1.
   fwdP = convolve(h, forward_kernelP, 'full')[n-1:2n-1], kernel P_f[n-1-d]=P[d]. For d=0..n-1. P_f = P[::-1]? Let's check: If P_f[n-1-d]=P[d], that's exactly P_f = P[::-1] (index n-1-d equals P[d] => P_f index i = P[n-1-i] = P_reversed? Wait P[::-1][i]=P[n-1-i]. We need P_f[n-1-d]=P[d]. Let i=n-1-d => P_f[i]=P[d]=P[n-1-i]=P[::-1][i]. So P_f=P[::-1]. Good. Convolve h with P[::-1] full, slice n-1:2n-1. Let's test simple P[d]=w at d=1. P_f index n-2=w. conv h*P_f index j+n-1: m such that Q[n-1-j? Wait earlier we wanted Q[n-1-d]=P[d], yes P_f. conv at r=n-1+j: ∑ h[m]P_f[r-m]. Let m=j+d? Then r-m=n-1-j? hmm trust previous. Let's test n=3, P[1]=w. P_f = [0,w? compute P=[0,w,0], P[::-1]=[0,w,0] symmetric. fwd should out[j]=w h[j+1] for j=0,1. conv h (a0,a1,a2) with Q[0]=0,Q1=w,Q2=0. full length5: index0=0,1=w a0,2=a1 w,3=a2 w,4=0. slice n-1=2 to 4: [a1w,a2w,a? index4? n-1:2n-1 => indices 2,3,4] = [a1w,a2w,0], not correct for j=0 (should a1w? out[0]=a1w), j=1 (a2w), j=2 (0) yes it's [a1w,a2w,0], matches out[0]=a1, out[1]=a2. Good; we don't need index 0. Good.
   backN similarly with N[0]=0.
   fwdN: N[::-1].
  G = suffixP + prefixN - backP - fwdP - backN - fwdN
But check prefixN[j] for j=0 should 0; N[0]=0 -> cumsum =0. For j>=a, cumulative includes N[a], good. suffixP includes P[0] plus positive future. Good.

   # positive shift d at center+d in our c: P_d = X[center-d]
   # negative N_d at center-d: N_d = X[center+d]
   c[center+1:] = X[center-1::-1]  # P_d for d=1..n-1
   c[center-1::-1]??? Need assign correctly.

   c = np.empty(2*n-1)
   c[n-1] = X[n-1]
   # for d=1..n-1:
   # positive shift d (P_d) at index n-1+d -> X[n-1-d]
   # negative shift d (N_d) at index n-1-d -> X[n-1+d]
   d = np.arange(1,n)

- `grad_full(h,alpha,mu)`:
   c=compute_cvals(h); m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); stable w.
   n=len(h); dx=2/n; center=n-1.
   P=np.zeros(n); N=np.zeros(n)
   P[0]=w[center]*dx
   for d 1..n-1: P[d]=w[center+d]*dx
   N[d]=w[center-d]*dx
   We can vectorize:
      P = w[center:].copy(); P *= dx
      N = np.zeros(n); N[1:] = w[:center][::-1] * dx
   suffix = cumsum(P[::-1])[::-1]
   prefix = cumsum(N) # N0=0
   Kback = P + 1j*N
   Kfwd = P[::-1] + 1j*N[::-1]
   convb = convolve(h, Kback)
   convf = convolve(h, Kfwd)
   G = suffix + prefix - (convb[:n].real + convf[n-1:].real + convb[:n].imag + convf[n-1:].imag)
   G += 2*mu*(h.sum()-n/2)
   return G
Check conv slice lengths: convb length 2n-1. Back (backward) result at indices 0..n-1. Fwd slice n-1..2n-2 length n. For direct np.convolve(h,Kback) with Kback complex, if h real. Good.

But note: For positive shift only, suffixP for P[0]=w, back/fwd as checked. For P[d]>0 suffix includes P[d] for j where future exists. Good. For N prefix: for N[a]=w, prefix[j]=w for j>=a. Good.

Optimization routines:
`lbfgs_full(h0, deadline, start_time, max_configs)`:
  h = project(h0)
  best_c = compute_c5(h)
  bounds=(0,1)*n
  for alpha,mu in configs:
     if time > deadline break
     try: res=minimize(smooth_obj, h, jac=smooth_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds, options={'maxiter': 3000, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':50})
     if time > deadline break after each maybe.
     new=project(res.x); c=compute; update; h=new.
  return h,best_c

Symmetric optimization:
Variables z length m. Need objective/grad top-level:
def sym_make_h(z):
   m=len(z); return np.concatenate((z, z[::-1]))

```
def project_h(h,n=None):
 if n is None: n=len(h)
 target=n/2.0
 h=np.array(h,dtype=float).reshape(n)
 np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
 for _ in range(100):
   s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
   if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
   if diff>0:
      room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      h += (diff/tot)*room
   else:
      tot=s
      if tot<1e-15: break
      h += (diff/tot)*h
 h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
 s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
 if abs(diff)>1e-12:
   if diff>0:
      idx=int(np.argmin(h))
      add=lambda? no lambda: add=min(1.0-h[idx], diff)
      while add < diff-1e-15: ... maybe need multiple.
   ...
```

```
def _adjust_sum_exact(h,target,n): # top-level
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-13:
   if diff>0:
      order=np.argsort(h)  # smallest have most room? Actually room=1-h, largest h has less room. argsort(-room)=argsort(x)
      for i in order:
         room=1.0-h[i]
         if room<=0: continue
         add=min(room, diff)
         h[i]+=add; diff-=add
         if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
   else:
      order=np.argsort(-h)
      for i in order:
         sub=min(h[i], -diff)
         h[i]-=sub; diff+=sub
         if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
 return h
```

Smooth objective functions top-level:
```
def smooth_objective(h, n, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_cvals(h)
    m = c.max()
    z = alpha*(c-m)
    zmax = z.max()
    if zmax > 500:
        val = m + (zmax + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zmax))))/alpha
    else:
        val = m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z)))/alpha
    val += mu*(h.sum()-n/2.0)**2
    return float(val)
```
But if c is complex? no. For alpha very large, exp underflow; stable.
`smooth_gradient(h, n, alpha, mu)`:
Need same stable weights; no need logsum value.
```
def smooth_gradient(h, n, alpha, mu):
    c=compute_cvals(h)
    m=c.max()
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    zmax=z.max()
    if zmax>500:
       w=np.exp(z-zmax); w/=w.sum()
    else: w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
    dx=2.0/n
    P=w[n-1:].copy()*dx
    N=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1: N[1:]=w[:n-1][::-1]*dx
    suffix = np.cumsum(P[::-1])[::-1]
    prefix = np.cumsum(N)
    Kback=P+1j*N
    Kfwd=P[::-1]+1j*N[::-1]
    cb=conv_full(h,Kback); cf=conv_full(h,Kfwd)
    G=suffix+prefix - (cb[:n].real + cb[:n].imag + cf[n-1:].real + cf[n-1:].imag)
    G += 2.0*mu*(h.sum()-n/2.0)
    return G
```
Check conv slices: If conv_full returns length maybe > due fft rounding? It returns exactly n+n-1. For direct, yes. If length maybe `2*n-1`; ensure cast: if `cb.shape[0] > n`, okay. If using fftconvolve, length can be n+n-1 but maybe with extra? No.
But for Kback, `cb[:n]` corresponds conv indices 0..n-1. For Kfwd, `cf[n-1:2n-1]`. If length maybe 2n-1. Good.
Potential problem: `np.cumsum(N)` prefix includes N[0]=0. For prefix[j] = sum_{d=1}^j N[d], correct. `suffix` includes P[0] + P[d] for d up to n-j-1? Since P length n. suffix[j]=sum_{d=0}^{n-1-j} P[d], correct.
Add penalty gradient: same as objective.

```
def sym_objective(z, m, alpha, mu):
    h=make_h_sym(z)
    return smooth_objective(h, 2*m, alpha, mu)
def sym_gradient(z, m, alpha, mu):
    h=make_h_sym(z)
    G=smooth_gradient(h, 2*m, alpha, mu)
    dz=G[:m] + G[m:][::-1]
    return dz
```

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-10
    np.random.seed(seed)
    parent = ...
    best_h = project_h(parent)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    # Sym parent
```

```
def random_smooth_z(m, seed, mode=0):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   if mode==0:
      # random Fourier cos with random coeffs
      x=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m  # 0..1 for left half
      y=0.5

   elif mode==1: binary blur
   elif mode==2: triangular peaks etc
   return z
```

```
def conv_full(x, y):
    n = len(x); m = len(y)
    if n == 0 or m == 0: return np.array([0.0])
    if n * m <= 400000:
        return np.convolve(x, y, mode='full')
    return sig.fftconvolve(x, y, method='auto')
```

Projection exact with while loops:
```
def project_h(h, n=None):
    if n is None: n=len(h)
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).reshape(n).copy()
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
       s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot < 1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=s
          if tot < 1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return adjust_sum(h,target,n)
```
`adjust_sum`:
```
def adjust_sum(h,target,n=None):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if n is None: n=len(h)
    for _ in range(2):
       s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: return np.clip(h,0,1)
       if diff>0:
          idxs=np.argsort(h) # smallest first (most room)
          for i in idxs:
             if diff<=1e-15: break
             room=1.0-h[i]
             if room<=1e-15: continue
             add=min(room, diff)
             h[i]=min(1.0, h[i]+add)
             diff -= add
       else:
          idxs=np.argsort(-h)
          for i in idxs:
             if diff>=-1e-15: break
             sub=min(h[i], -diff)
             h[i]=max(0.0, h[i]-sub)
             diff += sub
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Should be exact enough. For target feasible (0, n), okay.

`interpolate_to(h_old,n_old,n_new)`:
```
x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*2.0/n_old
x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*2.0/n_new
h_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_old)
return project_h(h_new,n_new)
```
Top-level.

`random_smooth_z(m, seed, kind=0)`:
```
def random_smooth_z(m, seed, kind=0):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  t=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m # in [0,1)
  if kind==0:
    z=0.5
    for f in range(1,6):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.02,0.25)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t + phase)
    z += 0.25*rng.normal(0,1,m)? Could use sinusoid enough.
  elif kind==1: # random binary blurred
    y=(rng.rand(m)<0.5).astype(float)
    # adjust to 0.5: sort
    if y.sum() < m/2:
       order=np.argsort(-y) # 1s first, set extras to1? Hmm easier: use previous project but need sum target; project handles sum via continuous not binary.
    return project_h(y,m)
  else: # smooth random via cumulative random walk
    r=rng.normal(0,1,m); r=r - r.mean(); z=0.5 + 0.5*r/np.max(np.abs(r)+1e-12)
  z=project_h(z,m)
  return z
```
For kind1, y binary sum not target; project makes fractional at extremes. Good.
For kind2, use cumulative: `r=np.cumsum(rng.normal(0,1,m)); r=r-(r.min()+r.max())/2; z=0.5+0.5*r/np.max(np.abs(r))`.

`binary_push_z(z)`:
```
def binary_push_z(z):
 m=len(z); zb=np.where(z>0.5,1.0,0.0)
 target=m/2.0; diff=target-zb.sum()
 if diff>0:
   order=np.argsort(-z)  # values closest to 1? Or largest first; set largest <1 to1
   for i in order:
      if zb[i]<1: zb[i]=1; diff-=1; if abs(diff)<1e-12 break
 else:
   order=np.argsort(z)
   for i in order:
      if zb[i]>0: zb[i]=0; diff+=1; if abs(diff)<1e-12 break
 return project_h(zb,m)
```
If diff not integer? target m/2 integer (m even target integer or .5 if m odd; we use even m target integer). diff integer. Good.

Optimization functions details:
```
def optimize_full(h0, deadline, configs=None, maxiter=800):
    h=np.array(h0,float); n=len(h); h=project_h(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h)
    if configs is None: configs=DEFAULT_FULL
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    for alpha,mu in configs:
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 25: break
       it = maxiter
       if n > 800: it = min(it, 500)
       if rem < 80: it = min(it, 250)
       try:
         res=minimize(smooth_objective, h, jac=smooth_gradient, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':it,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':30})
         h2=project_h(res.x)
         c2=compute_c5(h2)
         if c2<best_c: best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); h=h2.copy()
       except Exception: pass
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def optimize_sym(z0, deadline, configs=None, maxiter=800):

   best_h=make_h_sym(z); best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
   bounds=[(0,1)]*m
   for alpha,mu in configs:
      rem=deadline-time.time(); if rem<25 break
      it=maxiter; if m>400: it=min(it,500); if rem<80: it=min(it,250)

   return best_h,best_c,z
```

```
def slqp_obj(x, alpha):

    if zmax>500: return float(m + (zmax+log(sum(exp(z-zmax))))/alpha)
```

Active refinement (optional) top-level:
```
def active_refine(h, deadline, tol=1e-7, max_iter=200):
    h=project_h(h); best=compute_c5(h)
    n=len(h)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       c=compute_cvals(h); m=c.max()
       active = c >= m - max(1e-7, 1e-5*best)
       if not np.any(active): active=c==m
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[active]=1.0/active.sum()
       # Build gradient with exact weights similar to smooth_grad but no alpha
       G = grad_from_weights(h,w)  # top-level
       # normalize step size: Gscale = max(abs(G))*n? Gradient sum maybe; choose steps relative
       scale = max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(G))))
       for step in (0.1/scale,0.05/scale,0.02/scale,0.01/scale,0.005/scale,0.002/scale,0.001/scale):
          hnew=project_h(h - step*G)
          cnew=compute_c5(hnew)
          if cnew < best - 1e-14:
             best=cnew; h=hnew.copy(); break
       else: break
    return h,best
```
Need `grad_from_weights(h,w)` top-level (same as smooth_gradient but weights given). This can be reused for smooth_gradient to avoid duplicate code. Let's implement:
```
def grad_from_weight_array(h,w):
   n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; center=n-1
   P=w[center:].copy(); P*=dx
   N=np.zeros(n); if n>1: N[1:]=w[:center][::-1].copy(); N*=dx
   ...
   return G
```
Then `smooth_gradient` computes alpha weights and calls `grad_from_weight_array`, then adds penalty. Good. Active uses `grad_from_weight_array` directly.
But active_refine uses c from internal; weights normalized over active. Good.

Step 0.1 maybe too large? For h values [0,1], a step 0.1 okay. We can use raw steps (0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001) without scale. Better: compute `gmax=np.max(np.abs(G))`; if gmax>0, use steps = gmax? We want h change ~0.01. step=0.01/gmax. Let's do steps = [0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005]* (1.0/gmax if gmax>0 else 1). But if gmax very small, huge; cap change? Use raw if gmax<1e-6 break. def diversify_low_res(best_h, deadline):
```
    best_h=temp; best_c=compute
    n_best=len(temp)
    starts=[]
    for m in (24,48,96):
       h_small = interpolate_to(temp, n_best, 2*m) if n_best>2*m else project_h(temp,2*m)?
       h_small = symmetrize_h(h_small)
```

Let's define `final_downsample(h)`:
```
def final_downsample(h):
   best=h; bestc=compute_c5(h); n=len(h)
   candidate_ns=set()
   for factor in (2,3,4):
       nm=(n//factor)//2*2
       if nm>=80: candidate_ns.add(nm)
   for nm in (80,120,150,200,300,400,500):
       if 80 <= nm < n: candidate_ns.add(nm)
   for nm in sorted(candidate_ns):
       hs=interpolate_to(h,n,nm); c=compute_c5(hs)
       if c<bestc: best=hs; bestc=c
   return best,bestc
```

Now, let's attempt to prove gradient formula one more time with P,N suffix/prefix to avoid sign error in final. We derived:
For P_d positive: G = w[(1 - h_{j+d}) if j+d<n] - w h_{j-d} if j>=d.
Our formula for P only: suffixP[j] = w if j+d<n else0? If P[d]=w (single), suffix[j]=sum_{r=0}^{n-1-j} P[r]. It includes P[d] if d≤n-1-j -> j+d<n. Good. backP[j]=∑_{r=0}^j P[r] h[j-r] includes P[d] if d≤j -> w h[j-d]. fwdP[j]=∑_{r=0}^{n-1-j} P[r] h[j+r] includes P[d] if j+d<n -> w h[j+d]. G=suffix - back - fwd = w(1 - h[j+d]) - w h[j-d]. For j with both, correct. For j with neither, 0. Good.
For N_a negative: C = w[(1 - h[j-a]) if j-a≥0] - w h[j+a] if j+a<n. prefixN[j] includes N[a] if a≤j. backN[j]=∑_{r=0}^j N[r]h[j-r] includes N[a] if a≤j -> w h[j-a]. fwdN[j]=∑ N[r]h[j+r] includes if a≤n-1-j -> w h[j+a]. G=prefix - back - fwd = w(1 - h[j-a]) - w h[j+a]. Good.
Sum P,N independent. Good.

Suffix/prefix formulas with vectors:
`suffixP = cumsum(P[::-1])[::-1]` length n. For P single d, suffix[j]=P[d] for j ≤ n-1-d. Good.
`prefixN = cumsum(N)`. For N single a, prefix[j]=N[a] for j≥a? cumsum includes N[0]...N[j]; N[a] included if a≤j. Good.

`conv_full(h, Kback)`: Kback=d? For back, need conv index j: ∑ h[m]K[j-m] = ∑ K[j-m]h[m]. Let d=j-m -> K[d]h[j-d]. If K=P, backP[j]=∑ P[d]h[j-d]. Good for j=0..n-1. `cb[:n]` real part. Good.
`Kfwd = P[::-1]`; conv index r=j+n-1: ∑ h[m]P[n-1-(r-m)]? Let d=n-1-(r-m). Set m=j+? We want P[d]h[j+d]. Let r=j+n-1. Then d=n-1-(j+n-1-m)=m-j. So conv = ∑_m h[m] P[m-j]. Let d=m-j≥0: P[d]h[j+d], valid m=n? exactly. Good. Slicing r=n-1+j => `cf[n-1:2n-1]`. Good.
Similarly N. Great.

Now, `compute_cvals` orientation: We fill c[center+1:]=X[center-1::-1] (P_d), c[:center]=X[2n-2:center:-1] (N_d). Let's test n=4 maybe: X indices: P3 X0, P2 X1, P1 X2, C0 X3, N1 X4, N2 X5, N3 X6. c desired: c0=N3 X6, c1=N2 X5, c2=N1 X4, c3=C0 X3, c4=P1 X2, c5=P2 X1, c6=P3 X0. Our slices: c[4:7]=X[2::-1]=[X2,X1,X0] -> P1,P2,P3. c[0:3]=X[6:3:-1]=[X6,X5,X4] -> N3,N2,N1. Good.

Now, the final compute_c5 uses `np.correlate`; regardless orientation, max same. Need ensure our custom c max equals np.correlate max? It contains P_d,N_d; full correlate contains same set maybe. Yes.

Let's think about sum constraint exact in returned h: final `best_h=project_h(best_h, len(best_h))`, then `best_c=compute_c5(best_h)`. Good.

If final downsample returns h_small, we should project again and compute. `final_downsample` does compute c but project inside. It returns current. At run end, project.

Now, possible problem: `final_downsample` may choose a much lower n with lower c, but then sym/active not applied. If lower n has a local improvement, could further improve if allowed, but time. We can after downsample if time >15, run `active_refine` on selected lower n? But lower n small, cheap. Add:
```
if deadline-time.time() > 25:
  best_h,best_c=active_refine(best_h, min(time+20,deadline-10), max_iter=30)
```
After final project. Could improve low-n. Good.

Now, code size manageable? It will be long but within final token limit. Need avoid exceeding. We can trim some comments. The final answer should only code. It can be ~400 lines. Token budget okay.

`conv_full`:
```
def conv_full(x, y):
    n = len(x); m = len(y)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=float)
    if n * m <= 300000:
        return np.convolve(x, y, mode='full')
    return sig.fftconvolve(x, y, method='auto')
```
If y complex, product n*m int. `sig.fftconvolve` for complex maybe returns complex. Good.

`compute_cvals`:
```
def compute_cvals(h):
    n=len(h)
    dx=2.0/n
    b=1.0-h
    X=conv_full(h, b[::-1])
    X=np.real(X)
    need=2*n-1
    if len(X)<need:
       X=np.pad(X, (0, need-len(X)), mode='constant')
    elif len(X)>need:
       X=X[:need]
    c=np.empty(need, dtype=float)
    c[n-1]=X[n-1]
    if n>1:
       c[n:] = X[n-2::-1]
       c[:n-1] = X[2*n-2:n-1:-1]
    return c*dx
```
For n=1: need=1, c[0]=X[0]; slices skip. Good.

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full'))*dx)
```
For n=0? no.

`adjust_sum`:
```
def adjust_sum(h, target, n=None):
    h=np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if n is None: n=len(h)
    for _ in range(3):
        s=float(h.sum())
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff)<1e-13:
            break
        if diff>0:
            idxs=np.argsort(h)
            for i in idxs:
                if diff<=1e-15: break
                room=1.0-h[i]
                if room<=1e-15: continue
                add=room if room<diff else diff
                h[i]=h[i]+add
                diff-=add
        else:
            idxs=np.argsort(-h)
            for i in idxs:
                if diff>=-1e-15: break
                sub=h[i] if h[i]< -diff else -diff
                if sub<=1e-15: continue
                h[i]=h[i]-sub
                diff+=sub
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
```
`np.argsort(-h)` works.

`project_h`:
```
def project_h(h, n=None):
    if n is None: n=len(h)
    h=np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True).reshape(n)
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
       s=float(h.sum()); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=float(room.sum())
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=s
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return adjust_sum(h,target,n)
```
`reshape(n)` fails if h flat length n; okay. If h multi-dim? assume 1D.

`make_h_sym`:
```
def make_h_sym(z):
    return np.concatenate((z, z[::-1]))
```
If z empty? no.

`symmetrize_h`:
```
def symmetrize_h(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n % 2 == 1:
       h=interpolate_to(h,n,n+1)
       n=len(h)
    h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
    return project_h(h,n)
```
`interpolate_to`:
```
def interpolate_to(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*2.0/n_old
    x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*2.0/n_new
    h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
    return project_h(h_new, n_new)
```
If n_new<1? no.

`grad_from_weight_array`:
```
def grad_from_weight_array(h, w):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; center=n-1
    P=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    N=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    P[center:] = w[center:]
    P *= dx
    if n > 1:
        N[1:] = w[:center][::-1]
        N *= dx
    suffix = np.cumsum(P[::-1])[::-1]
    prefix = np.cumsum(N)
    Kback = P + 1j*N
    Kfwd = P[::-1] + 1j*N[::-1]
    cb = conv_full(h, Kback)
    cf = conv_full(h, Kfwd)
    # ensure slices
    if cb.size <= n:
       # shouldn't
       back_real = np.zeros(n); back_imag=np.zeros(n)
    else:
       back_real = np.real(cb[:n]); back_imag = np.imag(cb[:n])
    fwd_start = n-1
    fwd_len = n
    if cf.size < fwd_start+fwd_len:
       # pad?
       cf = np.pad(cf, (0, fwd_start+fwd_len-cf.size))
    fwd_real = np.real(cf[fwd_start:fwd_start+fwd_len])
    fwd_imag = np.imag(cf[fwd_start:fwd_start+fwd_len])
    G = suffix + prefix - back_real - fwd_real - back_imag - fwd_imag
    return G
```
If cb size less than n, pad similarly. Add robust:
```
cb = np.asarray(cb); cf=np.asarray(cf)
need=2*n-1
if cb.size<need: cb=np.pad(cb,(0,need-cb.size))
if cf.size<need: cf=np.pad(cf,(0,need-cf.size))
else if >need: trim.
```
To avoid branching each, trust length. But using FFT exact. Keep simple.
However, `np.real(cb[:n])` works if cb complex. If cb real (direct with real kernel? Kback complex so complex). Good.

`smooth_objective`:
```
def smooth_objective(h, n, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_cvals(h) if len(h)==n else compute_cvals(h) # n not needed
    m=float(c.max())
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    e=np.exp(z)
    val=m + float(np.log(e.sum()))/alpha
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff=float(h.sum()) - 0.5*n
        val += mu*diff*diff
    return float(val)
```
If alpha very high, z all -inf? At max z=0, e.sum ≥1. If c has nan? no.
`n` used for penalty; len(h) should equal n. For sym calls n=2m. Good.

`smooth_gradient`:
```
def smooth_gradient(h, n, alpha, mu):
    c=compute_cvals(h)
    m=float(c.max())
    w=np.exp(alpha*(c-m))
    s=float(w.sum())
    if s > 0: w = w/s
    else: w = np.zeros_like(c); w[len(c)//2]=1.0
    G=grad_from_weight_array(h, w)
    if mu != 0.0:
        G = G + 2.0*mu*(float(h.sum()) - 0.5*n)
    return G
```
Since z max=0, w not underflow all.

`sym_objective`:
```
def sym_objective(z, m, alpha, mu):
    h=make_h_sym(z)
    return smooth_objective(h, 2*m, alpha, mu)
```
`sym_gradient`:
```
def sym_gradient(z, m, alpha, mu):
    h=make_h_sym(z)
    G=smooth_gradient(h, 2*m, alpha, mu)
    return G[:m] + G[m:][::-1]
```

`optimize_full`:
Need use `configs` default maybe but to avoid none check each call. 
```
def optimize_full(h0, deadline, configs=None, maxiter=800):
    if configs is None: configs=CONFIGS_MAIN
    h=np.array(h0,dtype=float).copy()
    n=len(h)
    if n % 2 == 1:
       h=interpolate_to(h,n,n+1); n=len(h)
    h=project_h(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    for alpha,mu in configs:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 30: break
       it=maxiter
       if n>800: it=min(it,400)
       elif n>400: it=min(it,600)
       else: it=min(it,900)
       if rem<80: it=min(it,200)
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_objective, h, jac=smooth_gradient, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':it,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':30})
          h2=project_h(res.x,n)
          c2=compute_c5(h2)
          if c2 < best_c:
             best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); h=h2.copy()
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best_h, best_c
```
If `n` after interpolation changed, bounds correct. `res.x` might be nan; project clip. If c2 nan? compute returns nan; `c2 < best_c` false if nan? In Python, nan comparison false. Good.

```
def optimize_sym(z0, deadline, configs=None, maxiter=800):
   if configs is None: configs=CONFIGS_MAIN
   m=len(z0)
   z=project_h(z0,m)
   h=make_h_sym(z)
   best_c=compute_c5(h)
   best_h=h.copy(); best_z=z.copy()
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
   for alpha,mu in configs:
      ...
      rem=...
      it=maxiter
      nfull=2*m
      if nfull>1200: it=min(it,400)
      elif nfull>600: it=min(it,600)
      else: it=min(it,900)
      if rem<80: it=min(it,200)
      res=minimize(sym_objective,z,jac=sym_gradient,args=(m,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=...)
      z2=project_h(res.x,m)
      h2=make_h_sym(z2)
      c2=compute_c5(h2)
      if c2<best_c: update h,z
   return best_h,best_c,best_z
```

SLSQP functions:
`sum_eq`:
```
def sum_eq(x): return float(x.sum() - 0.5*len(x))
def sum_eq_jac(x): return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)
```
`optimize_slqp_full`:
```
def optimize_slqp_full(h0, deadline, alpha=30000.0, maxiter=60):
   h=project_h(h0); n=len(h)
   best_c=compute_c5(h); best_h=h.copy()
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq,'jac':sum_eq_jac}]
   it=maxiter
   if n>800: it=min(it,40)
   elif n>400: it=min(it,60)
   if deadline-time.time()<40: return best_h,best_c
   try:
      res=minimize(smooth_objective,h,jac=smooth_gradient,args=(n,alpha,0.0), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':it,'ftol':1e-15,'iprint':0})
      h2=project_h(res.x,n); c2=compute_c5(h2)
      if c2<best_c: return h2,c2
   except Exception: pass
   return best_h,best_c
```

`active_refine`:
```
def active_refine(h0, deadline, max_iter=40):
   h=project_h(h0); best_c=compute_c5(h); n=len(h)
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.time() >= deadline: break
      c=compute_cvals(h); m=float(c.max())
      tol=max(1e-8, 1e-5*max(1.0,best_c))
      active=c >= m - tol
      if int(active.sum()) < 3:
         order=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
         active_idx=order[:5]
      else:
         active_idx=np.nonzero(active)[0]
      w=np.zeros_like(c)
      w[active_idx] = 1.0/len(active_idx)
      G=grad_from_weight_array(h,w)
      gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(G))) if G.size else 0.0
      if gmax < 1e-12: break
      base=0.02/gmax
      steps=(base*1.0, base*0.5, base*0.25, base*0.125, base*0.0625, base*0.03125)
      improved=False
      for step in steps:
         hnew=project_h(h - step*G)
         cnew=compute_c5(hnew)
         if cnew < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c=cnew; h=hnew.copy(); improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         # Try a much smaller random-like? break
         break
   return h,best_c
```
If cnew nan, comparison false. Good. The step base=0.02/gmax means h change max 0.02. Good.

`shape_z`, `random_smooth_z`, `binary_push_z` as above. No closures.

`diversify_low_res(best_h, deadline)`:
```
def diversify_low_res(best_h, deadline):
    best_h=np.array(best_h,dtype=float).copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    n_best=len(best_h)
    starts=[]
    for m in (16,32,64,96):
       nm=2*m
       if n_best >= nm:
          h_small=interpolate_to(best_h,n_best,nm)
       else:
          h_small=interpolate_to(best_h,n_best,nm) # also works
       h_small=symmetrize_h(h_small)
       z=h_small[:m].copy()
       starts.append(z.copy())
       for kind in range(6): starts.append(shape_z(m,kind))
       for s in range(3):
          kind=s % 3
          starts.append(random_smooth_z(m, 1000+m*10+s, kind))
       starts.append(binary_push_z(z))
    scored=[]
    for z in starts:
       z=project_h(z, len(z))
       c=compute_c5(make_h_sym(z))
       if c < best_c + 0.05:
          scored.append((c,z))
    scored.sort(key=score_key_c)
    for c0,z in scored:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 25: break
       m=len(z)
       # quick optimize
       if rem < 60:
           configs=CONFIGS_SHORT[:2]
           maxit=150
       else:
           configs=CONFIGS_SHORT
           maxit=300
       if m > 80: maxit=min(maxit,200)
       try:
          h2,c2,z2=optimize_sym(z, deadline, configs, maxit)
       except Exception:
          continue
       if c2 < best_c:
          best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy()
          # continue maybe; new best lower, but low-res. If we update, starts list still old; okay.
    return best_h,best_c
```
Potential issue: In loop, if we update best_h to small (nm), subsequent scored c thresholds not used after; okay. But `n_best` for later? No, starts already generated. `optimize_sym` with deadline will run until deadline, not per-start! That's a bug: if we pass deadline absolute, first promising start could consume all time. We need give each start a limited end time. Use local deadline in function: `phase_deadline=deadline`; but for each start reduce. We can pass a `quick_deadline = min(time.time()+30, deadline)` to optimize_sym. But optimize_sym ends when its deadline reached. We need not use global deadline for all. In loop:
```
end_for_this = min(time.time() + 25.0, deadline)
h2,c2,z2=optimize_sym(z, end_for_this, configs, maxit)
```
Then next. Good.
Define `time_left = deadline - time.time()`; if >30, set 25 per.

Modify:
```
for ...:
   if deadline-time < 35: break
   end_this = min(time.time()+30.0, deadline)
   h2=optimize_sym(z, end_this, configs, maxit)
```
Good.

In phase1 in run, we set end=min(time+80, deadline-reserve). Good.

`final_downsample`:
```
def final_downsample(h0):
    best_h=np.array(h0,dtype=float).copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    n=len(best_h)
    candidates=set()
    for factor in (2,3,4,5):
       nm=(n//factor)//2*2
       if 80 <= nm < n: candidates.add(nm)
    for nm in (80,100,120,150,200,250,300,400,500,600):
       if 80 <= nm < n: candidates.add(nm)
    for nm in sorted(candidates):
       hs=interpolate_to(best_h, n, nm)
       c=compute_c5(hs)
       if c < best_c:
          best_c=c; best_h=hs.copy(); n=nm
    return best_h,best_c
```
If candidate downsampling best_h which is already lower n, n updates. Good.

Now, `run` details:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0 + float(budget_s) - 15.0
    np.random.seed(seed)
    try:
        h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    except Exception:
        n0=500
        x=(np.arange(n0)+0.5)*2.0/n0
        h0=np.abs(x-1.0)
    h0=project_h(h0)
    if h0.size % 2 == 1:
       h0=interpolate_to(h0,h0.size,h0.size+1)
    best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h0)
    # Stage 1
    if deadline - time.time() > 380:
       hsym=symmetrize_h(best_h)
       z=hsym[:len(hsym)//2]
       end1=min(time.time()+80.0, deadline-700.0)
       if end1 > time.time()+20:
          try: h2,c2,z2=optimize_sym(z,end1,CONFIGS_MAIN,800)
          except: pass
          if c2<best_c: best_h=h2.copy(); best_c=c2
       if deadline - time.time() > 320:
          hfull=best_h if best_h.size %2==0 else interpolate_to(best_h,best_h.size,best_h.size+1)
          end2=min(time.time()+60.0, deadline-500.0)
          if end2>time.time()+20:
             try: h3,c3=optimize_full(hfull,end2,CONFIGS_MAIN,800)
             except: pass
             if c3<best_c: best_h=h3.copy(); best_c=c3
    # low res
    if deadline - time.time() > 450:
       end_low=min(time.time()+130.0, deadline-380.0)
       if end_low>time.time()+30:
          try: h4,c4=diversify_low_res(best_h, end_low)
          except: pass
          if c4<best_c: best_h=h4.copy(); best_c=c4
    # high resolution
    n_high=1200
    if deadline - time.time() > 500:
       h_hi=interpolate_to(best_h, best_h.size, n_high)
       h_hi_sym=symmetrize_h(h_hi)
       z_hi=h_hi_sym[:n_high//2]
       end5=min(time.time()+300.0, deadline-220.0)
       if end5>time.time()+40:
          try: h5,c5,z5=optimize_sym(z_hi,end5,CONFIGS_MAIN,1000)
          except: pass
          if c5<best_c: best_h=h5.copy(); best_c=c5
    if deadline - time.time() > 350:
       h_hi2=interpolate_to(best_h, best_h.size, n_high)
       end6=min(time.time()+180.0, deadline-100.0)
       if end6>time.time()+40:
          try: h6,c6=optimize_full(h_hi2,end6,CONFIGS_MAIN,800)
          except: pass
          if c6<best_c: best_h=h6.copy(); best_c=c6
    # multistart high
    if deadline - time.time() > 250:
       h_sym_best=symmetrize_h(best_h)
       if len(h_sym_best)==n_high:
          zbase=h_sym_best[:n_high//2]
       else:
          h_tmp=interpolate_to(h_sym_best, h_sym_best.size, n_high)
          zbase=h_tmp[:n_high//2]
       m_high=n_high//2
       cands=[]
       cands.append(zbase.copy())
       cands.append(binary_push_z(zbase))
       for kind in range(6): cands.append(shape_z(m_high, kind))
       for s in range(6): cands.append(random_smooth_z(m_high, 7000+s, s%3))
       for mag in (0.02,0.05,0.10):
          zp = zbase + mag*(np.random.rand(m_high)-0.5)
          cands.append(project_h(zp,m_high))
       scored=[]
       for z in cands:
          z=project_h(z,m_high)
          c=compute_c5(make_h_sym(z))
          if c < best_c + 0.02:
             scored.append((c,z))
       scored.sort(key=score_key_c)
       for idx in range(min(4,len(scored))):
          if deadline - time.time() < 80: break
          _, z = scored[idx]
          end_this = min(time.time()+50.0, deadline-50.0)
          if end_this <= time.time()+10: break
          try: h7,c7,z7=optimize_sym(z,end_this,CONFIGS_SHORT,500)
          except: pass
          if c7<best_c: best_h=h7.copy(); best_c=c7
    # SLSQP moderate
    if deadline - time.time() > 140:
       n_slq=600
       h_slq=interpolate_to(best_h,best_h.size,n_slq)
       h_slq=symmetrize_h(h_slq)
       if len(h_slq)==n_slq:
          z_slq=h_slq[:n_slq//2]
       else: ...
       if deadline-time>100:
          h8,c8,z8=optimize_slqp_sym(z_slq, min(time.time()+60,deadline-50), alpha=30000.0, maxiter=60)
          if c8<best update
       if deadline-time>90:
          h_hi_s? maybe full slqp on best if len <=800
          if best_h.size <= 800:
             h9,c9=optimize_slqp_full(project_h(best_h), min(time.time()+60,deadline-40), alpha=30000.0, maxiter=50)
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sig
from scipy.optimize import minimize


CONFIGS_MAIN = (
    (20.0, 1000.0),
    (80.0, 1000.0),
    (300.0, 500.0),
    (1000.0, 200.0),
    (4000.0, 50.0),
    (12000.0, 10.0),
    (30000.0, 1.0),
)

CONFIGS_SHORT = (
    (50.0, 500.0),
    (300.0, 200.0),
    (1500.0, 50.0),
    (6000.0, 10.0),
)


def conv_full(x, y):
    n = len(x)
    m = len(y)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=float)
    if n * m <= 300000:
        return np.convolve(x, y, mode="full")
    return sig.fftconvolve(x, y, method="auto")


def compute_cvals(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    b = 1.0 - h
    X = conv_full(h, b[::-1])
    X = np.real(X)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if len(X) < need:
        X = np.pad(X, (0, need - len(X)), mode="constant")
    elif len(X) > need:
        X = X[:need]

    c = np.empty(need, dtype=float)
    c[n - 1] = X[n - 1]
    if n > 1:
        c[n:] = X[n - 2::-1]
        c[:n - 1] = X[2 * n - 2:n - 1:-1]
    return c * dx


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def adjust_sum(h, target, n=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if n is None:
        n = len(h)
    for _ in range(3):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idxs = np.argsort(h)
            for i in idxs:
                if diff <= 1e-15:
                    break
                room = 1.0 - h[i]
                if room <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                add = room if room < diff else diff
                h[i] = h[i] + add
                diff -= add
        else:
            idxs = np.argsort(-h)
            for i in idxs:
                if diff >= -1e-15:
                    break
                sub = h[i] if h[i] < -diff else -diff
                if sub <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                h[i] = h[i] - sub
                diff += sub
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def project_h(h, n=None):
    if n is None:
        n = len(h)
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True).reshape(n)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(100):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return adjust_sum(h, target, n)


def make_h_sym(z):
    return np.concatenate((z, z[::-1]))


def interpolate_to(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
    return project_h(h_new, n_new)


def symmetrize_h(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        h = interpolate_to(h, n, n + 1)
        n = len(h)
    h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_h(h, n)


def grad_from_weight_array(h, w):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    center = n - 1

    P = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    N = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    P[center:] = w[center:]
    P *= dx
    if n > 1:
        N[1:] = w[:center][::-1]
        N *= dx

    suffix = np.cumsum(P[::-1])[::-1]
    prefix = np.cumsum(N)

    Kback = P + 1j * N
    Kfwd = P[::-1] + 1j * N[::-1]

    cb = conv_full(h, Kback)
    cf = conv_full(h, Kfwd)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if cb.size < need:
        cb = np.pad(cb, (0, need - cb.size))
    elif cb.size > need:
        cb = cb[:need]
    if cf.size < need:
        cf = np.pad(cf, (0, need - cf.size))
    elif cf.size > need:
        cf = cf[:need]

    back_real = np.real(cb[:n])
    back_imag = np.imag(cb[:n])
    fwd_real = np.real(cf[n - 1:2 * n - 1])
    fwd_imag = np.imag(cf[n - 1:2 * n - 1])

    G = suffix + prefix - back_real - fwd_real - back_imag - fwd_imag
    return G


def smooth_objective(h, n, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_cvals(h)
    m = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(z)
    val = m + float(np.log(e.sum())) / alpha
    if mu != 0.0:
        diff = float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * n
        val += mu * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def smooth_gradient(h, n, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_cvals(h)
    m = float(c.max())
    w = np.exp(alpha * (c - m))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        w = w / s
    else:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[len(c) // 2] = 1.0
    G = grad_from_weight_array(h, w)
    if mu != 0.0:
        G = G + 2.0 * mu * (float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * n)
    return G


def sym_objective(z, m, alpha, mu):
    h = make_h_sym(z)
    return smooth_objective(h, 2 * m, alpha, mu)


def sym_gradient(z, m, alpha, mu):
    h = make_h_sym(z)
    G = smooth_gradient(h, 2 * m, alpha, mu)
    return G[:m] + G[m:][::-1]


def sum_eq(x):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * len(x))


def sum_eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)


def optimize_full(h0, deadline, configs=None, maxiter=800):
    if configs is None:
        configs = CONFIGS_MAIN
    h = np.array(h0, dtype=float, copy=True)
    n = len(h)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        h = interpolate_to(h, n, n + 1)
        n = len(h)
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    for alpha, mu in configs:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break
        it = maxiter
        if n > 800:
            it = min(it, 400)
        elif n > 400:
            it = min(it, 600)
        else:
            it = min(it, 900)
        if rem < 80.0:
            it = min(it, 200)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_objective,
                h,
                jac=smooth_gradient,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": it, "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-12, "maxls": 30},
            )
            h2 = project_h(res.x, n)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                h = h2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_h, best_c


def optimize_sym(z0, deadline, configs=None, maxiter=800):
    if configs is None:
        configs = CONFIGS_MAIN
    m = len(z0)
    z = project_h(z0, m)
    h = make_h_sym(z)
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_z = z.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m

    for alpha, mu in configs:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break
        nfull = 2 * m
        it = maxiter
        if nfull > 1200:
            it = min(it, 400)
        elif nfull > 600:
            it = min(it, 600)
        else:
            it = min(it, 900)
        if rem < 80.0:
            it = min(it, 200)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                sym_objective,
                z,
                jac=sym_gradient,
                args=(m, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": it, "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-12, "maxls": 30},
            )
            z2 = project_h(res.x, m)
            h2 = make_h_sym(z2)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_z = z2.copy()
                z = z2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_h, best_c, best_z


def optimize_slqp_full(h0, deadline, alpha=30000.0, maxiter=60):
    h = project_h(h0)
    n = len(h)
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    if deadline - time.time() < 40.0:
        return best_h, best_c
    it = maxiter
    if n > 800:
        it = min(it, 35)
    elif n > 400:
        it = min(it, 60)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_eq, "jac": sum_eq_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_objective,
            h,
            jac=smooth_gradient,
            args=(n, alpha, 0.0),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": it, "ftol": 1e-15, "iprint": 0},
        )
        h2 = project_h(res.x, n)
        c2 = compute_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            return h2, c2
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_h, best_c


def optimize_slqp_sym(z0, deadline, alpha=30000.0, maxiter=60):
    m = len(z0)
    z = project_h(z0, m)
    h = make_h_sym(z)
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_z = z.copy()
    if deadline - time.time() < 40.0:
        return best_h, best_c, best_z
    it = maxiter
    nfull = 2 * m
    if nfull > 1200:
        it = min(it, 40)
    elif nfull > 600:
        it = min(it, 70)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_eq, "jac": sum_eq_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            sym_objective,
            z,
            jac=sym_gradient,
            args=(m, alpha, 0.0),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": it, "ftol": 1e-15, "iprint": 0},
        )
        z2 = project_h(res.x, m)
        h2 = make_h_sym(z2)
        c2 = compute_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            return h2, c2, z2
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_h, best_c, best_z


def active_refine(h0, deadline, max_iter=40):
    h = project_h(h0)
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        c = compute_cvals(h)
        m = float(c.max())
        tol = max(1e-8, 1e-5 * max(1.0, best_c))
        active = c >= (m - tol)
        if int(active.sum()) < 3:
            order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
            active_idx = order[:5]
        else:
            active_idx = np.nonzero(active)[0]
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        if len(active_idx) == 0:
            break
        w[active_idx] = 1.0 / len(active_idx)
        G = grad_from_weight_array(h, w)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(G))) if G.size else 0.0
        if gmax < 1e-12:
            break
        base = 0.02 / gmax
        steps = (base, 0.5 * base, 0.25 * base, 0.125 * base, 0.0625 * base, 0.03125 * base)
        improved = False
        for step in steps:
            hnew = project_h(h - step * G)
            cnew = compute_c5(hnew)
            if cnew < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cnew
                h = hnew.copy()
                improved = True
                break
        if not improved:
            break
    return h, best_c


def shape_z(m, kind):
    t = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / m
    if kind == 0:
        z = 0.5 * np.ones(m, dtype=float)
    elif kind == 1:
        z = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    elif kind == 2:
        z = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * t)
    elif kind == 3:
        z = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(np.pi * t)
    elif kind == 4:
        z = 2.0 * np.abs(t - 0.5)
    else:
        z = 1.0 - 2.0 * np.abs(t - 0.5)
    return project_h(z, m)


def random_smooth_z(m, seed, kind=0):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    t = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / m
    if kind == 0:
        z = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        for f in range(1, 6):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.03, 0.28)
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + phase)
        z = 0.5 + z
    elif kind == 1:
        z = (rng.rand(m) < 0.5).astype(float)
    else:
        r = np.cumsum(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m))
        r = r - (r.min() + r.max()) * 0.5
        den = float(np.max(np.abs(r))) + 1e-12
        z = 0.5 + 0.5 * r / den
    return project_h(z, m)


def binary_push_z(z):
    m = len(z)
    zb = np.where(z > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).astype(float)
    target = 0.5 * m
    diff = target - float(zb.sum())
    if diff > 0:
        order = np.argsort(-z)
        for i in order:
            if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
                break
            if zb[i] < 1.0:
                zb[i] = 1.0
                diff -= 1.0
    else:
        order = np.argsort(z)
        for i in order:
            if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
                break
            if zb[i] > 0.0:
                zb[i] = 0.0
                diff += 1.0
    return project_h(zb, m)


def score_key_c(item):
    return float(item[0])


def diversify_low_res(best_h, deadline):
    best_h = np.array(best_h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    n_best = len(best_h)
    starts = []

    for m in (16, 32, 64, 96):
        nm = 2 * m
        h_small = interpolate_to(best_h, n_best, nm)
        h_small = symmetrize_h(h_small)
        z = h_small[:m].copy()
        starts.append(z.copy())
        for kind in range(6):
            starts.append(shape_z(m, kind))
        for s in range(3):
            starts.append(random_smooth_z(m, 1000 + 10 * m + s, s % 3))
        starts.append(binary_push_z(z))

    scored = []
    for z in starts:
        z = project_h(z, len(z))
        c = compute_c5(make_h_sym(z))
        if c < best_c + 0.05:
            scored.append((c, z))
    scored.sort(key=score_key_c)

    for c0, z in scored:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 35.0:
            break
        m = len(z)
        if rem < 70.0:
            configs = CONFIGS_SHORT[:2]
            maxit = 150
        else:
            configs = CONFIGS_SHORT
            maxit = 300
        if m > 80:
            maxit = min(maxit, 200)
        end_this = min(time.time() + 30.0, deadline)
        if end_this <= time.time() + 10.0:
            break
        try:
            h2, c2, _ = optimize_sym(z, end_this, configs, maxit)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def final_downsample(h0):
    best_h = np.array(h0, dtype=float, copy=True)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    n = len(best_h)
    candidates = set()
    for factor in (2, 3, 4, 5):
        nm = (n // factor) // 2 * 2
        if 80 <= nm < n:
            candidates.add(nm)
    for nm in (80, 100, 120, 150, 200, 250, 300, 400, 500, 600):
        if 80 <= nm < n:
            candidates.add(nm)
    for nm in sorted(candidates):
        hs = interpolate_to(best_h, n, nm)
        c = compute_c5(hs)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = hs.copy()
            n = nm
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 15.0
    np.random.seed(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    except Exception:
        n0 = 500
        x = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n0)
        h0 = np.abs(x - 1.0)

    h0 = project_h(h0)
    if h0.size % 2 == 1:
        h0 = interpolate_to(h0, h0.size, h0.size + 1)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    if deadline - time.time() > 380.0:
        hsym = symmetrize_h(best_h)
        z = hsym[:len(hsym) // 2]
        end1 = min(time.time() + 80.0, deadline - 700.0)
        if end1 > time.time() + 20.0:
            try:
                h2, c2, _ = optimize_sym(z, end1, CONFIGS_MAIN, 800)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_c = c2
                    best_h = h2.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

        if deadline - time.time() > 320.0:
            hfull = best_h
            if hfull.size % 2 == 1:
                hfull = interpolate_to(hfull, hfull.size, hfull.size + 1)
            end2 = min(time.time() + 60.0, deadline - 500.0)
            if end2 > time.time() + 20.0:
                try:
                    h3, c3 = optimize_full(hfull, end2, CONFIGS_MAIN, 800)
                    if c3 < best_c:
                        best_c = c3
                        best_h = h3.copy()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    if deadline - time.time() > 450.0:
        end_low = min(time.time() + 130.0, deadline - 380.0)
        if end_low > time.time() + 30.0:
            try:
                h4, c4 = diversify_low_res(best_h, end_low)
                if c4 < best_c:
                    best_c = c4
                    best_h = h4.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    n_high = 1200
    if deadline - time.time() > 520.0:
        h_hi = interpolate_to(best_h, best_h.size, n_high)
        c_hi = compute_c5(h_hi)
        if c_hi < best_c:
            best_c = c_hi
            best_h = h_hi.copy()

        h_hi_sym = symmetrize_h(h_hi)
        c_his = compute_c5(h_hi_sym)
        if c_his < best_c:
            best_c = c_his
            best_h = h_hi_sym.copy()

        z_hi = h_hi_sym[:n_high // 2]
        end5 = min(time.time() + 300.0, deadline - 220.0)
        if end5 > time.time() + 40.0:
            try:
                h5, c5, _ = optimize_sym(z_hi, end5, CONFIGS_MAIN, 1000)
                if c5 < best_c:
                    best_c = c5
                    best_h = h5.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    if deadline - time.time() > 350.0:
        h_hi2 = interpolate_to(best_h, best_h.size, n_high)
        end6 = min(time.time() + 180.0, deadline - 100.0)
        if end6 > time.time() + 40.0:
            try:
                h6, c6 = optimize_full(h_hi2, end6, CONFIGS_MAIN, 800)
                if c6 < best_c:
                    best_c = c6
                    best_h = h6.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    if deadline - time.time() > 250.0:
        h_sym_best = symmetrize_h(best_h)
        if len(h_sym_best) == n_high:
            zbase = h_sym_best[:n_high // 2]
        else:
            h_tmp = interpolate_to(h_sym_best, h_sym_best.size, n_high)
            zbase = h_tmp[:n_high // 2]
        zbase = project_h(zbase, n_high // 2)
        m_high = n_high // 2

        cands = []
        cands.append(zbase.copy())
        cands.append(binary_push_z(zbase))
        for kind in range(6):
            cands.append(shape_z(m_high, kind))
        for s in range(6):
            cands.append(random_smooth_z(m_high, 7000 + s, s % 3))
        for mag in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
            zp = zbase + mag * (np.random.rand(m_high) - 0.5)
            cands.append(project_h(zp, m_high))

        scored = []
        for z in cands:
            z = project_h(z, m_high)
            c = compute_c5(make_h_sym(z))
            if c < best_c + 0.02:
                scored.append((c, z))
        scored.sort(key=score_key_c)

        for idx in range(min(4, len(scored))):
            if deadline - time.time() < 80.0:
                break
            _, z = scored[idx]
            end_this = min(time.time() + 50.0, deadline - 50.0)
            if end_this <= time.time() + 10.0:
                break
            try:
                h7, c7, _ = optimize_sym(z, end_this, CONFIGS_SHORT, 500)
                if c7 < best_c:
                    best_c = c7
                    best_h = h7.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    if deadline - time.time() > 140.0:
        n_slq = 600
        h_slq = interpolate_to(best_h, best_h.size, n_slq)
        h_slq = symmetrize_h(h_slq)
        if len(h_slq) == n_slq:
            z_slq = h_slq[:n_slq // 2]
        else:
            z_slq = interpolate_to(h_slq, h_slq.size, 2 * (n_slq // 2))[:n_slq // 2]
        end7 = min(time.time() + 60.0, deadline - 60.0)
        if end7 > time.time() + 20.0:
            try:
                h8, c8, _ = optimize_slqp_sym(z_slq, end7, alpha=30000.0, maxiter=60)
                if c8 < best_c:
                    best_c = c8
                    best_h = h8.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

        if deadline - time.time() > 90.0 and best_h.size <= 800:
            end8 = min(time.time() + 50.0, deadline - 40.0)
            if end8 > time.time() + 20.0:
                try:
                    h9, c9 = optimize_slqp_full(project_h(best_h), end8, alpha=30000.0, maxiter=50)
                    if c9 < best_c:
                        best_c = c9
                        best_h = h9.copy()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    if deadline - time.time() > 50.0:
        end9 = min(time.time() + 35.0, deadline - 15.0)
        if end9 > time.time() + 8.0:
            try:
                best_h, best_c = active_refine(best_h, end9, max_iter=40)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if deadline - time.time() > 25.0:
        try:
            best_h, best_c = final_downsample(best_h)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if deadline - time.time() > 15.0:
        end10 = min(time.time() + 8.0, deadline - 3.0)
        if end10 > time.time() + 3.0:
            try:
                best_h, best_c = active_refine(best_h, end10, max_iter=8)
            except Exception:
                pass

    best_h = project_h(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
